# Experiment Plans

> Historical ledger. The canonical baseline, benchmark contract, and
> deprecation boundary are now defined by `README.md`,
> `baseline/manifest.json`, and `docs/DEPRECATIONS.md`. Entries below remain
> append-only evidence and are not active launch instructions.

Primary evidence target: use hard 9x9 Sudoku scaling as the fixed experimental
scaffold for jointly improving native FutureSeed and the next generation of
official-FLA linear recurrent memory, GDN3. The benchmark is not the product;
it is a controlled global-constraint task that exposes whether future context,
recurrent state capacity, and loop correction scale without solver-specific
repair, search, rules, or selectors.

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-GDN3-053` Redundant Independent-Address
GDN2 is complete and discarded. Exact pushed source `d569d3f` passes the
strict A800 contract with two distinct full K32xV32 address banks, one
pinned-official scan/layer, exact +67,592 parameters, doubled recurrent state,
all required gradients, bank-swap equivariance and active native FutureSeed.
The banks diversify strongly, but control/candidate balanced/future/past/joint
is `.494/.454/.534/.041` versus `.00875/.007/.0105/0`; errors rise
`2024->3965`. Swaps fall `1546->144` only through near-total retrieval collapse
and the candidate stays near chance for all ten epochs. Elapsed/post-warm/
warmed/allocation ratios are `2.032/2.033/2.139/1.531x`; active GPU samples
average `59.11%` SM and peak at `82%`. Independent `decouple key` redundancy
plus fixed mean read therefore breaks the native learning transition rather
than resolving ownership. Close bank/read/QK/init/training rescue; no Sudoku
transfer. Report:
`research/reports/experiments/gdn3-redundant-independent-address-mqar-20260815.md`.

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-DIAG-DUAL-001` is the sole next decision.
It freezes P-REPRO-001 and tests a non-deployable layer-0 upper bound: native
queries and erase keys remain unchanged, while the four known owner writes are
placed on the exact dual basis of the four owner-query directions through one
official DPLR scan. A tied-DPLR arm first proves algebraic replay stability.
The route opens only if balanced improves at least `.15`, errors fall `20%`,
at least half of wrong-key swaps repair and at least `80%` of native-correct
owners survive. A miss closes direct dual-address orthogonalization; no ridge,
layer, scale, key or training rescue. Report:
`research/reports/experiments/gdn3-dual-address-ceiling-diagnostic-20260815.md`.

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-DIAG-EDIT-001` is complete and opens one
separable-state route. Exact source `2b7fef75` passes the frozen baseline at
`3990/4000` agreement and `.4945/.4545/.5345/.041` balanced/future/past/joint.
Disabling the wrong owner's write at its value commit in layer0 repairs
`1519/1546` swaps (`98.25%`), identical to disabling both layers, while layer1
alone repairs only 48. The intervention retains just `5/1141` competing-owner
queries, proving a destructive storage tradeoff rather than a usable selector.
Erase-off both repairs `402/1546` but retains only `.7309` owner queries and
just `78` repairs with owner retained, so coherent erase ownership fails its
registered preservation gates. Decision: train one from-scratch redundant
independent-address state that stores every token in both banks and combines
reads without token routing. Do not build write/owner selectors, erase rescue,
or another global slot router. Active sampler utilization averaged `53.10%`
and peaked at `80%`; result SHA256 `e0d52470...d30a7b80`. Report:
`research/reports/diagnostics/native-mqar-edit-component-20260815.md`.

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-DIAG-OWN-003` is complete and closes head
fusion/readout selection. Exact pushed/read-back source `e535977a` passes the
registered fresh-process boundary with `3990/4000` frozen predictions,
`.4945/.4545/.5345/.041` balanced/future/past/joint and `2022/1546`
errors/swaps. A final-layer only-head label oracle repairs `516/1546` swaps
(`33.38%`) and reaches `.64825` balanced, proving that useful ownership
evidence is distributed across heads. The label-free max-margin proxy reduces
swaps to `1300` but collapses balanced accuracy to `.42325`, so the required
`+.02` quality gate fails; `441/1546` swaps also keep the same wrong owner in
every final-only variant. Do not build a selector, confidence router, head
count/mask/temperature table or inference oracle. The next formal candidate
must alter ownership in the live within-head recurrent transition. The
inference-only audit peaked at 80% SM and 1226 MiB; active samples averaged
43.90% SM. Result SHA256 is `03dd65df...650a9650ae`. Report:
`research/reports/diagnostics/native-mqar-head-ownership-20260815.md`.

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-GDN3-051` Causal Lagged-Address Commit is

Wrong-key swaps fall `1546->865` only because retrieval collapses, and disabling

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): a zero-parameter topology audit of frozen
P-REPRO-001 localizes the remaining error more sharply. Of `1546` valid-value
wrong-key swaps, `1539` (`99.55%`) stay inside the same future/past direction
class; only seven cross direction. `99.61%` choose the adjacent owner by write
rank, and `346` events form reciprocal two-cycles. The model therefore usually
retrieves the correct direction-specific value set but loses which of the two
same-direction instances owns each value. This rules out a global random
collision and explains why extra payload, protected FutureSeed copies, fixed
lag and generic side memory fail. The next intervention must target generic
instance ownership while preserving native retrieval; it must not reopen
decoupled-key, lag, cache, side-bank, Raven or readout families. Diagnostic:
`research/reports/diagnostics/native-mqar-swap-topology-20260815.md`.

`8be0f00c...e311a`. Report:

Current update (2026-08-15 CST): `P-REPRO-001` same-seed native GDN2 replay is
complete and passes every registered gate. Exact pushed/read-back source
`2791a7f4` serialized one initialization and ran two sequential
D128/L2/H4/K32/V32 native FutureSeed arms with identical data, warmup,

past/joint `.494/.454/.534/.041`, exactly the same trained parameter hash,

rescue. Decision SHA256 is `df8ae1c2...ae98854`. Report:
`research/reports/experiments/native-gdn2-repro-mqar-20260815.md`.

Current update (2026-08-14 CST): `P-FS2-009` Metric-Pullback FutureSeed is
complete and discarded. Its strict contract proves exact identity for full
outputs, terminal states, nonzero incoming state and all parent gradients;
two official GDN2 backward paths remain, while the zero-parameter pullback is
active, finite and equivariant. Candidate versus private-Log-SPD control raises
balanced/future/past/joint from `.1385/.1485/.1285/0` to
`.17825/.1820/.1745/.002` and reduces errors `3446->3287`, but misses every
registered absolute and relative quality floor. More importantly, wrong-key
valid-value swaps rise `602->748` and their error fraction
`.17470->.22756`. All cost gates pass (`.945/.940/1.127/1.001x` for
elapsed/wall/warmed/allocation). Metric pullback therefore improves some state
readability without resolving address binding; close direction, transpose,
inverse, scale, metric and training rescue. The next successor must target
collision/state organization rather than another cross-layer metric map.

Current update (2026-08-14 CST): `P-GDN3-037` completed and is discarded. Its

Current update (2026-08-14 CST): `P-GDN3-031` completed and is rejected. It is
the clean-room direct test of the `GDN_decouple_k` mathematical idea at external
commit `c7667fd11d95d3d147f59bb3d4492989909b69ae`; the unlicensed repository's code
was not copied. The candidate retained native GDN2 decay/channel erase/write,
Q/V/output, K32xV32 state, native FutureSeed and one live scan, while a tied-copy
erase-key projection and Triton short convolution made initialization exactly
native. The strict A10080 contract passed with two official GDN2 carriers, two
official `ChunkDPLRDeltaRuleFunctionBackward` nodes, exact tied key/state/output
parity, finite gradients, zero state/scan delta and bounded sampled transition.
After the fixed 10-epoch L1024 run, however, balanced/future/past/joint are only
`0.011/0.008/0.014/0`, versus historical `0.7475/0.7415/0.7535/0.339` and the
locked current-runtime `0.30625/0.3115/0.3010/0`. Errors rise `2775->3956`.
The two key branches strongly diverge: layer cosines are `0.0483/0.0600`
(separation `0.9517/0.9400`, relative RMS `1.3796/1.3711`). The apparent
wrong-key swap-fraction gain `0.458018->0.035642` is not binding closure: 91.66%
of all errors collapse into the broad value-token range and predictions are
dominated by tokens 165/177 (`1914/1743` of 4000 queries). Cost and stability
pass, so the negative is specifically scientific: unconstrained erase/write
specialization stops erase from addressing the rows populated by write/read.
No Sudoku transfer ran. Close direct key decoupling without angle/tie/scale,
initialization, regularization, kernel or training rescue; decision SHA256
`1b631993bbc5dd1e483bd0178083b93f0b9f10c358208ebc7cd767156ba8e538`.

Current update (2026-08-13 CST): `P-DIAG-ADDR-001` is preregistered as a
read-only, zero-parameter GPU diagnostic on the completed P-FS2-007 surprise
K16 directional-MQAR L1024 checkpoint. It does not train or change logits. On
the same 1,000 validation cases it compares receiver normalized query Q against
the four producer committed-write K addresses (the value token at
`write_position+1`) and against receiver-native reprojected K, while scoring
each target write by the exact official committed-edit norm. The fixed first
128 cases additionally measure producer/receiver key-Gram geometry, exact
target-address survival through later decay/erase, and matched surprise-K16
versus recency-K16 survival. This complements rather than reruns P-BIND-001's
bit-exact Sudoku loop evidence (`0.5230` correction AUROC, `0.4792/6.4568/402.91`
effective-rank/anisotropy/condition medians).
Outputs are top-1/MRR/margin by direction, correctness, wrong-key swap and
surprise bucket, plus geometry, survival correlations and ordinary-eval cost.
Bitwise first-batch logit parity, unchanged parameter SHA and all gradients
`None` are mandatory.
The fixed branch rule selects receiver-native address/cache only for >=0.10
rank gain with swap/error>=0.70; selects loop/readout convergence only when
receiver-native top1 is already >=0.80 overall and >=0.60 on errors; otherwise
kills the address/cache line unless low target survival and anisotropic geometry
jointly select live-state address interference. Diagnostic/ordinary time and
allocation ratios must be <=3.00/1.50; the slower FP32 survival replay is
diagnostic-only and fixed to 128 cases.
R1 `004dd055` exited before model construction because its launcher omitted the
external Zoology source root from `PYTHONPATH`; zero GPU memory was allocated
and the generated `abort.json` is non-science evidence. R2 changes only the
environment wiring and adds an exact Zoology SHA check; all diagnostic gates
remain frozen. R2 then loaded the exact checkpoint but its observer incorrectly
required the K16 replay to appear in the `chunk_gdn2` hook. Official FLA routes
that short replay through its recurrent path, so only the producer and receiver
L1024 scans are visible there. R3 requires those exact two captures and directly
asserts production top16 equality, same-object producer-to-receiver evidence,
and finite nonzero replay terminal/seed state. Metrics, cost and branch gates
are unchanged.

Current update (2026-08-13 CST): `P-FS2-006` is closed without a quality
verdict at its strict recurrence-parity gate. P-GDN3-007 already falsified a
generic controller that reads inherited state before the scan, so this test is
narrower: at each receiving layer and the receiver's own normalized position-Q
address, compare the frozen inherited FutureSeed read `q_t^T F` with the
post-write live read `q_t^T S_t`. The contrast `d_t=q_t^T F-q_t^T S_t` is
RMS-matched to the exact official committed edit and enters only as a virtual V
residual. Discovery forms one global `[11,H,V]` descent direction from three
independent hard ranges; a disjoint holdout tests fixed `+/-` direction,
frozen-only and a cross-call-coherent fixed board-shuffled sham at a causal 1% actual committed-edit RMS
target over heads/values at each current token, with actual BF16 relative error
capped at 10% and no future-token normalization.
The parent training sampler was replayed for all 384,000 draws and its final
Python RNG state exactly matches the checkpoint. This leaves 3,451,620 strict
unseen 51-64 rows; each range contributes 128 discovery plus 128 disjoint
holdout boards. R1 was intentionally stopped before science when review found
that its shuffled-F donor changed across receiver calls. R2 fixes one coherent
donor per microbatch and passes source/GPU/data/wheel checks, but the first
official comparison gives FP32-replay output/terminal relative RMS
`0.002523/0.001143`; output misses the preregistered `<=0.002` parity ceiling.
No discovery direction, holdout score or quality verdict exists. Close this
replay-based diagnostic without tolerance, precision, budget, batch, seed or
duration rescue; receiver-live recommit itself remains untested.

Current update (2026-08-13 CST): `P-GDN3-028` completed and is rejected.
Atomic shared-payload addressing reduces wrong-key valid-value swaps among

Current update (2026-08-13 CST): `P-LOOP-003` completed and rejects the final

Therefore P-LOOP-004 is not admitted. Close phase-gate, projection, scalar

Current update (2026-08-12 CST): `P-GDN3-025` is discarded after its sole
registered endpoint. R4 passed exact same-weight zero/nonzero-state identity,

indexed committed-delta bank is weaker than improving the main recurrent

protected-row behavior. The exact step3001 probe is active, diverse and
bounded.

`0.025391->0.023438`, and official51-55/56-60/61-64 loop5 blank deltas are
`-0.000179/-0.001510/-0.008455`. Same-board late correction improves only on

Previous update (2026-08-07 CST): `P-GDN3-011` orthogonal head-write routing is
discarded at its exact step3001 production stability gate. Pushed clean SHA
`d093d21` passed the strict official-FLA identity, nonzero incoming-state,
single-call graph, two-stage gradient, parameter, permutation and synthetic
geometry contract. The production probe then activated all 12 routes, with
loop5 angle abs `0.059469` and routed-V relative RMS `0.037769`, but its FP32
routed/base norm max error reached `5.1444e-4`, above the fixed `1e-4` limit.
The route's explicit FP32 casts were still inside CUDA autocast, a boundary the
synthetic direct checker did not cover. The preregistered fail-close rule
forbids an autocast/precision rescue; no formal continuation started.

P011 preserved the original 81-token, one-call official-GDN2 path.
For each token it constructs one permutation-equivariant two-dimensional plane
over the eight recurrent heads from shared V32 descriptors, predicts one
zero-initialized invariant angle, and rotates the eight V32 write payloads in
that plane before the unchanged official recurrence. The rotation is exactly
identity at zero angle and preserves aggregate cross-head payload Frobenius
norm for every signed angle. It changes which independent recurrent head
receives each token's evidence without adding state, scan, another core, or a
task rule. The fixed candidate adds exactly `12*32*3 = 1,152` parameters.

This tests a missing topology rather than another residual strength: existing
heads update isolated K32xV32 states even though the block output later mixes
heads. P005 changes within-head gate coherence, P006 adds a parallel state,
P007 conditions writes on inherited state, P008 adds a post-scan transition,
and P009/P010 test transition composition; none allows token-dependent evidence
routing among the existing live recurrent heads inside the original scan.
The strict contract and exact-resume migration passed, but the registered
production norm-preservation gate did not. This is a systems result rather
than Sudoku evidence: an operation can use `.float()` tensors yet still be
autocast to lower precision unless the precision boundary itself is asserted
under the production context. No angle/plane/descriptor/target/precision,
seed/LR/loss/batch/width/depth/duration rescue is authorized. The next
high-information candidate must be a different scalable memory topology, not
a nearby orthogonal-router repair.

This closes physical token expansion as an exact-resume mechanism on the

evaluation, so P010 has no benchmark score, checkpoint or visualization. The
contract log SHA is `840b1562...713988b`; abort SHA is
`fae2959f...fe42985`. GPU1 returned naturally to 0 MiB. Do not rescue auxiliary
gate/decay, order/count, rank, projection, scale, identity tolerance, seed, LR,
loss, batch, width/depth or duration. The next high-information successor must

Previous update (2026-08-07 CST): `P-GDN3-009` orthogonal chunk-state transport
is discarded at its strict CUDA contract. P008 showed that post-scan
transition depth moves all three hard blank ranges but does not close boards
and allows state magnitude to explode. P009 therefore tested stable address
transport inside the live scan.

P009 splits the existing 81-token official GDN2 call only at its native
64-token chunk boundary. A head-shared `V32 -> 2*K32+1` controller uses the
first-chunk output summary to define an orthonormal K-row plane and signed
angle; it applies an exact two-plane rotation to the live KxV state, then the
remaining 17 tokens continue through the unchanged pinned official chunk. The
angle row is zero-initialized, so migration is an exact identity; every opened
rotation preserves Frobenius norm. It adds exactly 24,960 parameters, no state,
tokens, scan, second core, reverse traversal, or task logic. This directly
tests content-conditioned, norm-stable within-scan memory transport rather than
another pre/post-scan residual. Quality gates remain `+0.02` hard macro exact
or the registered `+0.03` mixed alternate. Stability additionally requires
boundary norm ratio within `[0.9999,1.0001]` and terminal RMS within `4x`
control; cost ceilings were `+40%` elapsed and `+30%` allocation. Pushed clean
SHA `1994db5` passed zero-angle full-output and all 12 terminal-state identity
checks, including nonzero incoming state. It then exposed only one
`ChunkGDN2FunctionBackward` per final terminal state instead of the registered
two. This fails the differentiable live-state transport contract, so no
step3001 probe or formal continuation started. Do not rescue the graph check,
boundary, custom backward, controller, scale, or training settings. The next
mechanism must stay within one differentiable official recurrent call or prove
an official-compatible state-gradient path before any continuation.

Previous update: `P-CAUSAL-021` is complete. The data audit
found that P019/P020 did not train on full
WikiText-103: the train JSON contains only the first 20,000 source rows and
produces 10,003 grouped windows. Their reported 20.48M input tokens therefore
come from 16 different corruptions of the same 10,000 windows, only about
1.28M independent WordPiece tokens. P021 keeps D128/L4, parameters, optimizer,
seed, batch, 1,250 steps and 20.48M total input tokens fixed, but replaces that
reuse with 160,000 independent windows and one corruption. This 16x data
diversity intervention at identical compute changes causal/FutureSeed accuracy
from P020's `0.27668/0.28427` to `0.27583/0.29249`: the paired delta is
`+0.01666`, CI `[+0.00973,+0.02364]`. CE advantage grows
`0.09948->0.16867`, a `+0.06919` gain that passes the registered `+0.05`
mechanism gate, while net token repairs grow `36->79`. Independent text
diversity was a real bottleneck. The strong paper gate still misses
(`+0.03` accuracy or `0.20` CE), so do not claim language-quality parity and
do not run a data-size table. The positive endpoint slope authorizes one clean
next run that scales independent data and total compute together.

Previous update: `P-CAUSAL-020` is complete and closes the
small-depth language scaling line. At fixed WordPiece data, D128 state width,
optimizer, seed and 20.48M tokens, L4 creates three active FutureSeed routes.
Causal/FutureSeed accuracy is `0.27668/0.28427` (`+0.00759`, paired interval
fully positive) and CE is `5.32369/5.22421` (`0.09948` improvement). Suffix
removal cost rises from L2 `0.43915` to L4 `0.58652`, and net token repairs rise
`12->36`, so added routes are genuinely used. But CE advantage grows only
`+0.01213` over L2, missing the registered `+0.05` depth-amplification
requirement and both strong gates. One transfer was not the primary bottleneck.
Do not run L3/L6/L8, longer steps, LR, mask, freeze-policy, loss or seed rescue.
Retain P019/P020 as real-text mechanism evidence, not a language-quality claim;
a future headline needs a materially larger pretraining regime or a different
established carrier.

## A. Current Candidate Plan

Current update (2026-08-02 CST): `P-PCOND-001` is discarded after its sole

baseline; revisit causal write preconditioning only with a fused kernel or on a
direct online-memory interference/retrieval benchmark.

`docs/MILESTONE_GDN2_ADDRESS_PAYLOAD.md` and tagged as
`milestone/gdn2-address-payload-v1-20260731`. This marks the mechanism as the
strongest current GDN2 carrier modification, not as solved hard Sudoku or a
universal GDN2 replacement. The next proof remains one clean from-scratch,
matched-initialization/data/compute comparison; no address/loss/seed table is
authorized by the milestone.

Current update (2026-07-31 15:04 CST): `P-ADDR-004` is complete and does not
authorize another short continuation. Step9300->9600 clean 51-64 hard-stage
training lowers CE `0.9661->0.9460` and raises mean official hard blank
`0.5437->0.5800` (`+0.0364`). Exact first opens to `0.0059` in both 51-55 and
56-60 entirely through later loops, but misses the `0.02` primary gate and the
`+0.04` blank-only partial gate; 61-64 exact remains zero. Paired cases show
both real closure (`4->0` final errors), a 64-blank `29->10` improvement, and a
64-blank `23->34` regression. Clean compute helps on average, but this capacity
does not converge uniformly. Stop 300-step duration stacking. The next
justified experiment must scale generic state/model capacity or independent
data coverage from matched initialization, not tune loss/seed/address.

Current update (2026-07-31 14:20 CST): `P-ADDR-004` is the sole active
scaling-law gate. The matched step9300 comparison already proves the
position-Q/K advantage is persistent; the remaining question is whether the
positive CE/blank slope can become full-board closure under more clean
hard-stage data. Resume the exact candidate step9300 checkpoint for exactly
300 unchanged 51-64-stage steps. Continue this line only if official hard exact
opens and later loops improve it, or if mean hard blank rises by at least
`+0.04`. Kill at step9450 if CE remains above `0.95` and improves by less than
`0.03`. No control, seed, address, loss, or duration table.

Current update (2026-07-31 14:16 CST): `P-ADDR-003` is complete and strongly
passes its matched-total-compute gate. At identical step9300, parameter count,
data, official case hashes, random-order curriculum, FutureSeed, five-loop
supervision, and strict official FLA chunk/Triton path, canonical position-Q/K
raises mean official 51-64 loop5 blank accuracy from `0.2753` to `0.5437`
(`+0.2683`) and lowers train CE from `1.5707` to `0.9661`. On matched
64-blank batch69, control wrong cells change
`53->50->50->51->51`, while position-Q/K changes
`32->21->18->15->15`. The advantage is persistent carrier learning plus real
loop correction, not 200 extra steps. Exact remains zero in every separate hard
range, so the global-closure problem is still open. Keep address/payload
separation; stop address/loss/seed tables. The next justified scale run must
train matched normal-GDN2 and position-Q/K from the same initialization under
more data/model/compute.

Current update (2026-07-31 13:10 CST): `P-ADDR-003` is the sole active
fairness gate. `P-ADDR-002` already established a large equal-compute step9100
address/payload advantage, then continued only the positive candidate to
step9300. To determine whether that later gap is persistent rather than merely
200 extra optimizer steps, resume the exact normal-GDN2 control step9100 state
for exactly 200 steps under the unchanged random-order contract. Position-Q/K
must retain at least `+0.10` mean official hard blank accuracy, lower train CE,
and stronger loop correction at matched step9300 to remain a paper candidate.
If the gap is below `+0.05`, downgrade the result to transient optimization
speed and stop this direction. No second seed or hyperparameter table.

Current update (2026-07-31 12:44 CST): `P-ADDR-002` is complete and survives
as a strong mechanism result, but not yet as a solved-task result. At the
equal-compute step9100 gate, canonical-position Q/K raises mean official
51-64 blank accuracy from `0.2037` to `0.4333` versus matched content-entangled
GDN2, with identical boards, parent checkpoint, continuation batches,
optimizer budget, parameters, FutureSeed, loop loss, and official FLA kernel.
All hard-range exact rates remain zero. The preregistered positive candidate
then continues alone to step9300: mean hard blank accuracy reaches `0.5437`,
train CE reaches `0.9661`, and mixed loop1->5 exact changes
`0.00195 -> 0.02344`. On matched 64-blank batch 110, wrong cells fall
`37 -> 23 -> 15`, while the stopped control is `48 -> 48 -> 49`. This is
real recurrent correction and a positive clean-scaling slope, but every
separately sampled official 51-64 range still has zero exact. Keep generic
address/payload separation; do not claim closure, do not compare the
step9300 candidate to the step9100 control as same compute, and do not sweep
address modes, loss weights, or seeds. A future paper gate requires one
same-total-compute control and candidate scale run.

`P-OCC-001` is discarded. The strict official KDA occurrence-rotary candidate
improves the final in-distribution occurrence but misses the causal OOD gate:
length512/repeat16 accuracy is `0.0313` versus `0.0371` content baseline
(`-0.0059`). Standard occurrence RoPE changes recency preference but does not
make overwritten older values queryable. Do not implement the learned
key-local counter or sweep rotary/base/beta/seed/width/length; revisit only
with qualitatively different versioned state capacity.

Current update (2026-07-30 18:02 CST): `P-ADDR-001` strongly survives its
falsifier. On the same 256 official 51-64-blank boards, row-major loop5 reaches
`0.2617` exact / `0.6970` blank accuracy, while reverse, column, box, and
fixed-random traversal all fall to `0` exact and at most `0.1839` blank
accuracy. Every content token retained the same absolute position embedding
and outputs were restored before scoring, so this is traversal dependence, not
missing position metadata. `P-ADDR-002` is now the only active mechanism
experiment: use canonical position embeddings for GDN2 Q/K addresses, keep
hidden content responsible for V/decay/erase/write/output gates, and train both
this model and a matched normal-GDN2 control under the same randomized cell
orders from the same step9000 checkpoint. No row/column/box rules, new slots,
new parameters, or kernel fallback are allowed.

`P-OCC-001` is a separate generic-memory falsifier motivated by KDA's
fine-grained decay. It does not replace or modify the Sudoku experiment:
channel-wise decay can encode age, while repeated writes to one semantic key
need a query-matchable occurrence address. Both matched KDA arms receive the
same additive occurrence metadata; only the candidate binds that metadata into
Q/K with a parameter-free rotation. Oracle success is required before spending
any work on a learned key-local counter.

Current update (2026-07-30 15:25 CST): `P-FSMC-001` is discarded after its
single preregistered matched GPU1 probe. Exact SHA `2867c889` passed all six
FIR math tests, pinned official FLA GDN2 reference/adapter/FutureSeed-stack
checks, exact identity output/state/input/state/core gradients with and
without initial state, and both exact-checkpoint smokes. The mechanism is
active: final hazard TV ratio is `0.9839`, learned `rho=0.0990`, lag mass is
`0.1475`, and relative hazard change is `0.0111`. It does not improve the
primary hard distribution. Mixed loop5 exact changes `0.2402 -> 0.2500`, but
official 51-55/56-60/61-64 changes
`0.3594/0.1387/0.1641 -> 0.3535/0.1465/0.1621`, for hard-range mean delta
exactly `0.0000`. Full-model wall overhead is `+7.19%`; peak memory overhead
is `+20.65%`, also just outside the fixed systems gate. Paired visualizations
show a real solved-case rescue (`19 -> 0` wrong cells) and a similarly large
regression (`3 -> 26`), while the hardest shared case worsens for both arms
after loop1. Smooth forgetting changes which solution basin wins; it does not
create reliable recurrent convergence. Close decay smoothing. Do not sweep
K, rho, seed, LR, loss, width, or continuation length.

Current update (2026-07-30 01:27 CST): `P-GAIN-001` is discarded. It asked
whether GDN2's channel-wise erase gate is spending
decay on useful forgetting or on non-normal one-step amplification. The
decay-funded projection preserves effective erase strength and compresses only
the anisotropic component; it adds no parameters and keeps the official
rank-one kernel. The parent checkpoint is pinned by SHA256
`606caf522959...` and source SHA `42102bd65a28...`. First require
pure-Torch/SVD, official FLA 9c8e42e
chunk/fused/backward, no-budget bitwise, checkpoint, and benchmark gates. Then
run exactly one matched step9000->9100 `external_identity` vs
`decay_funded` comparison on GPU1. Both formal arms use the same external FP32
normalization and official kernel; only the candidate projects the erase gate.
SHA `c4bcbbad` passed the complete preflight and the formal control completed,
but the candidate stopped before its first step when a large real batch put
the closed-form FP32 projection a few ulps beyond its strict certificate. This
is a numerical-contract failure, not a quality result. SHA `755d5871` fixed
the boundary but recomputed the full certificate twice, so ABCCBA correctly
rejected its `+23.33%` projection overhead before training. The sole authorized
retry reserves half the existing FP32 certificate tolerance before the
closed-form projection, computes the certificate once, and falls to the
isotropic endpoint only when that numerical reserve consumes all feasible
slack. SHA `ac566f96` passed math and all CUDA correctness gates, but measured
`+20.98%`, still just above the fixed `20%` gate. The final systems retry
removes no mathematical check: it combines feasibility, delta preservation,
and spectral validity into one fail-closed device reduction and removes a
redundant finite-tau branch. A lease-restarted run of that implementation
measured `+26.46%`; together with the earlier `+0.17%`, this exposes the
single-iteration cold-allocator timer as unstable. The threshold remains
`20%`, but its instrument now times five warmed forward/backward calls per
ABCCBA point and measures cold-cache memory separately. It also persists raw
samples on failure. SHA `56030a13` passed that stable systems gate at
`+14.56%` time and `+18.46%` memory, plus all checkpoint smokes. Its formal
control completed, but the candidate exposed another production-batch FP32
rounding violation before step1. The final numerical retry selects the
analytic `lambda=0` endpoint for exactly those post-certificate violators
without a second dense pass; the actual BF16 gate is independently audited
after cast. SHA `4715a5a8` passed every correctness gate, but multiple
K-dimensional endpoint selections pushed stable time overhead to `+28.10%`
(`+18.49%` memory), so it stopped before checkpoint smoke. The sole remaining
implementation retry makes the endpoint decision entirely in scalar
token/head certificate space and constructs the K-dimensional gate once. The
actual FP32 erase strength and BF16 recurrence gate remain independently
audited. Exact SHA `e4c929dd` again passed 24/24 math tests and every official
FLA CUDA correctness gate, but its stable ABCCBA benchmark measured `+32.14%`
time and `+18.49%` memory over matched identity. This exceeds the fixed `20%`
systems gate, so formal candidate training was not launched and `abort.json`
was written. The earlier same-semantics two-step smoke also showed the
mechanism is not gentle: it clipped `99.996%` of rows, sent `56.40%` to the
isotropic endpoint, retained mean anisotropy scale `0.019`, and changed paired
51-55 loop5 exact `0.625 -> 0.125`. Close strict `c=1` gain budgeting. Do not
sweep cap, seed, LR, loss, width, or length.

Current update (2026-07-29 13:05 CST): `P-FS2-005` is the one approved
FutureSeed2 probe. Raw multilevel EMA, state-basis transport, scalar/content
gates, and same-layer block carry are already rejected. The new candidate
keeps FutureSeed1 intact and asks a genuinely different question: can a
two-layer-old GDN2 terminal state help after the producer layer reads it back
through its own pretrained query/RMSNorm/output coordinates? One zero-init
scalar per two-hop edge makes launch bit-exact to FutureSeed1. Allow one GPU1
contract, two-step full-stack smoke, and matched step9000->9100 continuation;
do not sweep hop count, scale, seed, LR, loss, or length.

| P-DIAG-027 | discarded | 当前 native FutureSeed 把每层 terminal state 强制归一到 unit RMS 后再注入下一层。这个稳定了训练，却可能抹掉 state magnitude 中与样本难度、证据强弱相关的一维通用信息。若 hard-board 全局一致性需要这条信息，一个初始化严格等价于旧模型、但能根据 raw state RMS 学习有界幅度的 FutureSeed 应比固定 unit normalization 更稳；若 gain 不产生样本差异或 hard exact 不涨，则 state magnitude 不是缺失信息。 | 新增唯一机制 `FUTURE_SEED_NORM_MODE=adaptive_rms`：仍保留 state 方向归一化，只用每个 sample/head/layer 的 `log(raw_rms)` 经过可学习 slope+bias 产生 `exp(0.5*tanh(.))` gain，范围约 `0.61-1.65`，参数全零初始化时与旧 unit norm 完全等价。记录 raw RMS 和 gain mean/std。只跑一条从 P-DIAG-021 step12000 续训到 step12600 的 D224/L12/H14/D16、GDN expand-v4、loop5/all-loop CE、effective batch128 probe；checkpoint 12300/12600，holes53/60/64。无新 loss、noise、scratch、repair/search/selector、seed sweep。 | GPU1 only | completed 2026-07-11 12:05 CST | 成功：gain std `>=0.01` 且 holes60/64 至少一项 loop5 exact `>=0.25`，或 official `56-64 >=0.17` while `51-55 >=0.33`；失败：gain std `<0.002`、gain 撞边界、或 step12300 hard exact/blank 明显跌出 P-DIAG-021 regime，则停止并判定 magnitude channel 不是全局一致性的缺口。 | Discarded. Final loop5 exact is `0.2109` vs P-DIAG-021 `0.2363`; official `51-55` is `0.3418` vs `0.3555`, and `56-64` is `0.1367` vs `0.1582`. Loop remains useful (`0.0234 -> 0.2109`), but adaptive magnitude loses throughout. Do not sweep gain range/init/LR/seed: one scalar state magnitude is not the missing global-consistency information. |

| P-DIAG-003 | done | P-DIAG-002 证明完整 RWKV+native FutureSeed 在 official Sudoku 上不是整体不行，而是在 `51+` blanks 出现断崖。现在要区分：GDN+FutureSeed 是否也有同样 blank-count cliff，还是 GDN state 本身比 RWKV 弱到连低 blank bucket 也无法 solved。 | 单条 GDN+native FutureSeed 分桶诊断：official Sudoku arrays，D192/L10/loop5，1000 steps，`OFFICIAL_EVAL_BLANK_RANGES=46-50,51-55,56-64`，case-bank 可视化。不跑 no-FS，不扫 seed/width/loss，不改模型。 | GPU1 only | ~10m | 若 GDN+FS 在 46-50 高 exact、51+ exact 归零，则 cliff 是 FutureSeed+loop 当前全局一致性瓶颈；若 46-50 也差，则 GDN state/backbone 是额外瓶颈。 | Done. Decision after correction: archived negative/side probe only; do not use right-to-left scan as FutureSeed. |
| P-EQR-014 | discarded | P-EQR-013 的 off-mainline CUDA/Triton bidirectional scan replacement 在早期 matched eval 赢了；关键问题是这个优势会不会随训练预算扩大转成 official EqR Sudoku exact，还是只会形成稳定但错误的状态吸引子。 | 单条 scale gate，不做 seed/hidden/bias 表：沿用 official EqR Sudoku 数据、h192/head6/batch128、Triton scan backend，分别训练 official mixer-base 和 bidirectional scan mixer replacement 到 e1024/step7168，然后用同一 bounded official eval (`max_eval_steps=16`, `noise_scale=0.01`, `different_init=1`) 对比。 | GPU1 only | ~20m train+eval continuation | 继续 scan-mixer 支线：scan 在 e1024 仍保持 accuracy/loss 优势或打开 exact；停止：official mixer-base 打开 exact 而 scan 卡住，或 scan 只表现为低 residual 的错解。 | 长预算反转，discard 同配置继续训练。step448 scan acc `0.1881` vs base `0.0955`，e256 scan `0.4207` vs base `0.3668`；但 e1024 base `acc=0.6644/exact=0.0249/lm_loss=0.7664`，scan `acc=0.4231/exact=0/lm_loss=1.4029`。scan residual16 更低 (`3.065` vs `4.979`) 不是成功，而是稳定错吸引子。This is not evidence about the real FutureSeed algorithm. |
| P-EQR-012 | done | P-EQR-011 给出强 residual 信号但 exact 未开：FutureSeed 是否只是低预算轨迹扰动，还是能在更长 matched-compute 下把 cheap causal backbone 更快推向官方 Sudoku exact？ | 只跑一条 longer scale gate，不跑 seed：official EqR cheap causal Sudoku，`arch.mlp_t=false attention_causal=true h192 heads6 halt16 noise0.01 batch128 epochs256`，比较 causal-cheap vs reverse-causal FutureSeed。继承官方数据和 eval；禁止 selector/search/repair/Sudoku rule。按低 ROI 规则截断：cheap 和 FS 都只保留 paired step250/500 metrics，避免继续跑官方重 eval。 | GPU1 only | truncated after paired step500 | 如果 FutureSeed residual 优势转成更早 nonzero exact 或至少更好 convergence/loss，就继续 cheap-bidirectional story；若 exact 仍 0 且 loss 不赢或 residual 优势消失，停止这条 claim。 | Mixed but not enough. Step250 reproduces strong residual signal: FS accuracy `0.09956` vs cheap `0.09318`, residual16 `83.34` vs `167.76`, exact both `0`, loss worse. By step500, exact still `0`, FS token acc delta shrinks to `+0.0010`, loss remains slightly worse, and residual16 advantage shrinks to `111.65` vs `121.45`. Decision: stop epoch/seed scaling for this exact injection; next needs analysis of residual-to-exact mismatch or a generic state/readout change. |
| P-EQR-011 | done | 旧 official EqR FutureSeed patch 只是 H/L latent cross-injection；在 causalized EqR 下没有新的 future-token source，因此不是“FutureSeed cheap 实现双向”的强检验。强 EqR baseline 补完后，下一条 FutureSeed 实验应直接测试 cheap bidirectional source，而不是继续扫旧 patch 的 gate/seed。 | 在官方 EqR codebase 上准备一个最小 generic bidirectional-FutureSeed variant：baseline 是官方非因果 EqR released/checkpoint 作为 strong ceiling；训练对照是 causal/cheap mixer backbone vs causal/cheap mixer + future-token seed source，使用同一官方数据和 eval。只跑一个高信息 probe，优先 Sudoku sample-efficiency，因为 Maze path-weight proxy 已经多次证明会 broad-mask 中性。禁止 selector/search/repair/Sudoku rule；成功后再扩到 Maze 可视化。Patch implementation now exists as `apply_bidir_futureseed_patch.py`: reverse the token sequence body, run one causal attention+MLP seed block, flip back, and add it to input embeddings before recurrent recursion. Static validation applied it cleanly to pristine upstream SHA `aba94e9`. Critical config guard: for official Sudoku, force `arch.mlp_t=false`; otherwise `attention_causal=true` is a no-op because the upstream Sudoku config uses a noncausal token-MLP mixer. | GPU1 only after P-EQR-010 | one quick pair | 成功标准不是 token acc 小涨，而是 causal/cheap backbone 加 FutureSeed 在官方 Sudoku quick eval 上明显更快接近 EqR ceiling，或同等 exact 下计算更低；若只复现旧 cross-level neutral 结果，判定旧 patch 不是 paper 主机制。 | Mixed mechanism signal. At h192/head6/epochs64, both arms exact `0.0`; FS token acc improves `0.09317->0.09966`, but eval loss is slightly worse. The important signal is recurrent state contraction: `residual_of_16_steps` drops `167.90->82.85` under matched cheap causal backbone. This supports a single longer scale gate, not a seed sweep. |
| P-MAZE-009 | done | 上轮 D256/L12 pure scale 已证明 loop16 能在不制造 FN 的情况下剪掉约 `44` FP/case，但仍卡在 broad mask。一个更 bitter-lesson 的下一轴不是手写规则，而是扩 RWKV recurrent state 本身：固定 `d_model=256`，把 `heads=8, head_dim=32` 改成 `heads=4, head_dim=64`，每层 state matrix capacity 从 `8192` 增到 `16384`。如果当前瓶颈是 recurrent state 太窄，state64 应该让 loop16 继续压 FP/pred_path_frac，而不是只复制宽 mask。 | 只跑一条 clean FutureSeed+loop state-capacity scale probe：官方 Maze30，RWKV FutureSeed，D256/L12，train loops8/eval loops16，activation checkpoint，batch64，lr1e-4，path-weight CE only，steps800；不加 DAT、boundary、budget decoder、selector、search、repair 或 maze rule。batch64 因速度低 ROI 后降到 batch32 viability probe，不做细粒度 batch/seed/weight 表。 | GPU1 only | ~1.5h | state64 相比上轮 state32 的最低有效信号：step200/300 loop16 FP drop 明显超过 `44`/case，pred_path_frac 降到 `<0.65` 且 FN 不明显增加；若仍 `F1<=0.32`、pred_frac `>0.65`，说明 broad-mask 主要是 objective/decision-state 问题，不是 RWKV state size。 | Mixed positive mechanism result. b64 compiled and used GPU1 but was too slow: only step1 after `~15.5m`. b32 reached step200 and strongly improved the operating point versus state32 scale: loop16 F1 `0.4686` vs `0.3144`, pred_frac `0.4322` vs `0.7097`, FP `269.7` vs `519.4`, FN `0`. But loop gain stayed exactly `0`, so state capacity helps one-pass path coverage/calibration, not late-loop correction. head_dim64 is slow; next scale axis should use head_dim32 with more heads/width or a simple recurrent decision state. |

Seed0: top1 `0.991699`, top4 exact `0.986816`, majority exact `0.987305`, any-correct `0.992676` over 2048 examples, matching README quick target. Full 423,168-example paper eval is a separate 8-GPU ~33.6h run and was not run under GPU1-only constraint. |
| P-MAZE-008 | discarded | Maze 也许不是缺新机制，而是官方 Maze30 的 900-token grid 需要更大的纯 compute 才能从 high-recall broad mask 过渡到真路径选择。如果 bitter lesson 在这里成立，放大 width/depth/train loops/eval loops 后，loop16 应该自然减少 false positives，而不是继续复制 loop1。 | 只跑一条 clean FutureSeed scale gate：官方 Maze30，RWKV FutureSeed，D256/L12，train loops8/eval loops16，path-weight CE only；不加 DAT、boundary、budget decoder、selector、search、repair 或 maze rule。no-checkpoint b24/b16/b15/b14 先定位 fit 边界，随后用 activation checkpoint b64 作为可行 scale 点。 | GPU1 only | ~1h | 继续 scale 方向的最低门槛：loop16 相比 loop1 FP 下降 `>=20` 且 recall `>=0.85`，或 loop16 F1 `>=0.55` 且 pred_path_frac 不靠膨胀；否则 pure scale on official Maze30 暂判不足 | Mixed but not enough. No-checkpoint D256/L12 train8/eval16 OOMed even at batch14. Activation-checkpoint b64 with lr3e-4 hit NaN at step100. Stabilized b64 lr1e-4 gave a real loop-pruning signal at step100/200: loop16 FP `564.3->520.2` and `563.2->519.4` with FN `0`, gain `~+0.017`; but loop16 pred path frac stayed `~0.71`, F1 plateaued `0.3140->0.3144`. Decision: pure scale alone does not solve Maze broad-mask; next needs a generic decision/training-state change, not more batch fit. |
| P-MAZE-007 | done | DAT 机制已判负，但上轮暴露了更基础的问题：早停会丢掉 hard-case loop 可视化，导致 broad-mask 失败形态不可复核。继续做 Maze 前必须保证失败也留下可分析证据。 | 在 `rwkv_maze_probe.py` 加通用 broad-mask early-abort 出口：触发后先写 `abort.json`、partial `rwkv_maze_probe.json`、README 和 input/target/loop1/4/8/16 hard-case HTML。随后只跑一条严格阈值 FS DAT probe 验证归档链路，不把分数当机制正证据。 | GPU1 only | <20m | 产出 abort metadata + hard-case visuals；证明后续 kill-rule 实验不会再丢 qualitative evidence | Infrastructure success, model negative. `rwkv-maze-abortviz-probe-20260622` aborted at step100 by `broad_mask_low_precision_no_fp_drop` and wrote `abort.json`, partial metrics, README, and visual HTML. Metrics confirm broad-mask copy: loop1/loop16 F1 `0.4614/0.4614`, precision `0.3006/0.3006`, recall `1.0/1.0`, FP `272.1->272.1`, FN `0->0`. |
| P-EQR-006 | discarded | FutureSeed 的论文主张应该是 cheap bidirectional information mechanism：在官方 EqR codebase 中，它应能替代或压缩 EqR mixer 的信息交换，而不是作为完整 mixer 之后的无归因 patch。 | 官方 EqR matched-compute gate：先复现不改语义的 EqR mixer baseline，再比较一个贡献可分离的 FutureSeed replacement/compression 版本。当前 probe 选择 causalized EqR mixer：把 EqR token mixer 改为 causal attention，再比较 causal noFS vs causal+FutureSeed，并用既有 full noncausal EqR pathw8 baseline 作边界参照。必须同数据、同训练预算、同评估脚本，并报告 exact/token acc/path F1/precision/recall/pred_path_frac/FP/FN/速度/显存/参数量和 hard-case visuals。 | GPU1 only | e256 pair | path F1 `>= +0.03` 且不靠 broad mask 膨胀，或同等 F1 下训练/推理计算节省 `>=20%` | Negative but useful. `official-eqr-causal-fs-gate-20260622`: causal base loop16 path F1 `0.468067`; causal+FutureSeed `0.468157`, delta `+0.000090`. Precision/recall/pred frac are effectively unchanged (`0.3063/1.0/0.4327`), FP stays about `270` per case, and loop gain remains tiny. FutureSeed shows a step500 optimization edge but no final path-aware win. Decision: do not sweep causal gate; next only mixer-compression or cleaner bidirectional proxy. |
| P-MAZE-005 | discarded | Maze failure may be a decision-boundary/calibration bottleneck: the causal RWKV may rank true PATH cells above false positives, but raw argmax cannot convert that ranking into a sparse mask. | Add a generic learned path-budget head and learned-budget decoder to the RWKV Maze runner. Train only with supervised path fraction, then decode PATH as top model-scored cells under the model's own predicted budget; compare no-FutureSeed vs FutureSeed under matched compute. | GPU1 A100 | ~45m | budget decoder reduces FP/pred frac without recall collapse; FutureSeed improves budgeted F1 or recall at matched budget | Negative but informative. The count head learned true PATH fraction accurately (`abs_err≈0.0095-0.0098`), but budget decoding overpruned true path cells: noFS budget loop8 F1 `0.3387`, FS `0.3289`, with FN about `78-80` per case. Raw loop gain stayed near zero. Conclusion: total path mass calibration is not enough; Maze bottleneck is true-vs-false PATH ranking / late-loop correction. |

Official FlashAttention kernel is blocked on this host by glibc/nvcc mismatch; PyTorch SDPA fallback reproduces the reported exact metrics. |

FS minus no-FS loop16 F1 only `+0.0008`, and both loop gains are `-0.0003`. DAT loss is trainable but converges to the same broad-mask fixed point; do not sweep corruption/weight/seed. |
